## Corpus Check
- 7166 files · ~8,944,050 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 55867 nodes · 115336 edges · 4121 communities (2378 shown, 1743 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 93% EXTRACTED · 7% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7809 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `37822ce6`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
